# Additional Information


## Reference

Data Storage and Processing
Your vehicle is fitted with electronic control units. Data is processed and generated by control units based on inputs from sensors. Your vehicle’s control units are used to keep the vehicle safe, support you while driving (driver assistance systems), and to enable convenience and infotainment functions.
In certain circumstances, the data generated or processed by control units can be considered personal data under the law. Depending on the vehicle data available, this data may reveal information about your driving behavior, your location, or your route, as well as your usage patterns.
Within your infotainment screen, you can also configure how VinFast Connected Services transmit your data and location.
To configure privacy settings:
Tap on your profile name on the status bar > My Profile > Privacy Settings
International personal data transfer
VinFast has offices in Vietnam, Europe, United States and some other countries and regions in the world. Depending on your use of VinFast Products, your personal information may be transferred and/or stored in or accessed from multiple countries, including but not limited to Vietnam, Europe, United States, Singapore and other locations where we or our service providers maintain servers with relevant contractual clauses to protect such transfers.
rights for VinFast Connected Services or for activities where VinFast is the data controller
Your personal data is processed according to applicable data protection law.
VinFast may be required to provide data gathered by VinFast to government agencies according to legal requirements, such as police investigations of criminal offenses.
A government agency may also require access to data from vehicles themselves in individual cases under applicable law. For example, if there is an accident, the airbag control unit may help clarify the situation by providing information.
If you believe your rights have been infringed as a result of the processing of your personal data, you have a right to lodge a complaint with the competent data protection authority.
Data Privacy
We place a high priority on protecting your data privacy. Details about how we manage your data can be found in our Privacy Policy. For requests to manage your data, including requests to delete your data, please refer to our DSR form.

## VinFast eStore

VinFast eStore Overview (SOTA)
VinFast eStore (also known as VF eStore) is a smart connectivity platform integrated into VinFast’s electric vehicles. This system creates a technological ecosystem that allows users to easily interact with their vehicles through a mobile app and internet connectivity.
VinFast eStore enables you to:
Manage and monitor your vehicle
Control your vehicle remotely
Use smart features and in-car entertainment services
VinFast eStore Packages
Basic: Free plan with essential features
Pro: Adds remote control and security features
Premium: Expands entertainment options and personalization
Prime: The most advanced plan, including in-car internet/Wi-Fi
For more details about VinFast eStore, please visit:   VinFast eStore | VinFast
Remote Control
The remote vehicle control feature allows users to manage and operate their vehicle anytime, anywhere via a smartphone. You can monitor vehicle status, perform actions such as locking/unlocking doors, turning on the air conditioning, and receive security alerts quickly and conveniently.
Usage Requirements
Customers need to activate a VF eStore package that supports remote control (Upper/Pro, Premium, Prime, etc.).
For details:   VinFast eStore | VinFast
Users must download the VinFast app and update it to the latest version. To download the VinFast App, please scan the QR code below:
Androi
IOS
The vehicle must be updated to the latest FRS version with VF eStore available.
How It Works
After activating the VF eStore package on the vehicle
Users can control certain vehicle functions through the VinFast mobile app.
Key Features
Remote Control
Users can remotely control the following features (*):
Lock/Unlock the car
Open the trunk
Open the windows
Control the air conditioning
Turn on lights
Activate the horn
Camp mode
(*) Note: Features may vary depending on the vehicle model.
Notifications
Anti-theft alerts: Similar to the VinFast app. If the vehicle shows signs of movement or unauthorized access, the smartwatch will notify the driver.
Vehicle notifications: Users receive basic notifications and vehicle status updates similar to those in the VinFast app.
Additionally, users receive updates about charging status, battery information, and remaining driving range.
Smartwatch Usage Guide
The Smartwatch feature allows users to conveniently control and monitor their vehicle directly from their smartwatch. It enables basic operations without needing to use their phone (if the vehicle supports it), such as: locking/unlocking the car, opening the trunk, turning on the air conditioning, lights, horn, etc. It also provides important notifications and alerts from the vehicle (anti-theft, vehicle status, battery level, remaining range, etc.).
Usage Requirements
Customers must activate the VF eStore package that supports remote control features on the vehicle (Upper/Pro, Premium, Prime, etc.).
Supported operating systems: Wear OS and watchOS.
The smartwatch must be connected to the phone via Bluetooth before use.
Users need to download the VinFast app and update it to the latest version.
The vehicle must be updated to the latest FRS version with VF eStore available.
Users should wear the smartwatch to enable features and receive notifications.
How It Works
After activating the VF eStore package on the vehicle.
Connect the phone to the smartwatch via Bluetooth.
For Wear OS, download the VinFast app from the supported app store.
Check the VinFast app—if the status shows “Connected,” the smartwatch can now be used to control the vehicle.
Recommended distance for the smartwatch to receive signals from the vehicle: less than 10 meters.
Key Features
Remote Control
Users can remotely control the following features (*):
Lock/Unlock the car
Open the trunk
Open the windows
Control the air conditioning
Turn on lights
Activate the horn
Camp mode
(*) Note: Features may vary depending on the vehicle model.
Notifications
Anti-theft alerts: Similar to the VinFast app. If the vehicle shows signs of movement or unauthorized access, the smartwatch will notify the driver.
Vehicle notifications: Users receive basic notifications and vehicle status updates similar to those in the VinFast app.
Additionally, users receive updates about charging status, battery information, and remaining driving range.
App Store (Application Marketplace)
The in-vehicle app store allows users to download and use additional entertainment and utility applications directly on the central display. This helps enhance the overall in-car user experience.
Common applications include:
Watching movies
Listening to music
Playing games
Other utility apps
Usage requirements:
Customers must activate a VF eStore service package that supports (such as Premium, Prime packages, etc.) in order to use the app store.
Nap Mode (If available)
This feature is to provide users a relaxing and energy-restoring short sleep experience. When activated, Nap mode optimizes the car’s interior environment by adjusting various system such as seat, ambient lighting, climate control and sound insulation to create an ideal atmosphere for a quick nap
Activation
Nap mode can be activated by using:
Car mode on the infotainment
VF eStore Premium/Prime
Preconditions for nap mode activation:
The vehicle is not in ACC/OFF state.
Gear Park (P) is selected.
All doors are closed.
Parking brake is applied.
Battery level is greater than 30 percent.
High-voltage battery is in normal state.
Air conditioner is in normal state.
To activate Nap Mode on the infotainment:
Tap Car mode on the Infotainment Screen.
From vehicle mode selection list, tap Nap Mode .
Configure Nap duration, Sound.
Tap Start Nap Mode
To configure nap duration:
Go to Car mode > Nap mode > Nap duration
Select 15/ 20/ 30/ 45/ 60 (min)
To configure sound during nap:
Go to Car mode > Nap mode > Sound
Select None/ White noise/ Pink noise.
When activated, Nap Mode shall take actions as belows:
Close all windows for safety.
Air conditioner recirculation is set to inside. Every 15 minutes, circulation is switched to outside for 5 minutes and reverted back to inside afterwards.
Temperature is set to 22 Celcius degree linearly from current value in 5 minutes.
Fan speed is set to level 1 after 10 minutes.
Reduce notification sound.
Keep playing media or play noise depends on settings.
Automatically decrease the media volume gradually over time to 5 percent in 15 minutes.
Turn off dome light.
Keep the infotainment screen’s brightness in 10 seconds, then dim brightness to 5 percent in 15 seconds.
NOTE
If users actively change media volume, the automatic media volume adjustment process shall be canceled.
If users actively change climate, the automatic climate adjustment process shall be canceled.
Whenever user touches the screen, infotainment screen brightness shall be restored to the value before activating Nap mode.
If screen brightness received any changes, the infotainment shall set it as starting value for Nap mode.
User profile can not be switched when Nap mode is activated.
Deactivation
To deactivate/exit Nap Mode manually:
Tap Exit Nap Mode widget on the Infotainment screen.
Conditions to deactivate Nap Mode automatically:
Time out for Nap mode.
Battery level falls below 20 percent.
Air conditioner is malfunction.
High-voltage system is malfunction.
Gear is shifted out of Park (P).
Any door is opened.
When deactivated, Nap mode shall take actions as belows:
Raise an alarm to wake user up when Nap mode is deactivated automatically.
Undone scheduled tasks are cancelled.
Restore infotainment screen brightness to the value before activating Nap mode.
Stop playing white noise if playing.
Turn OFF Reduce Chime mode.
Climate will be kept as current.
Smart Assistant (Vivi 3.0)
The Smart Assistant is one of the features of VF eStore, delivering a smart, convenient, and personalized interaction experience for users. With its ability to understand and respond flexibly, the assistant makes vehicle usage easier and more modern.
Usage Requirements:
Customers must activate a VF eStore service package that supports Smart Assistant features to use this functionality.
The vehicle must have a stable Internet connection (Wi-Fi or mobile data).
Key Features:
Proactive assistance: Provides reminders and suggestions based on user habits, context, and preferences.
Advanced vehicle control: Enables natural, fast, and accurate interaction with the vehicle.
Personalized experience: Supports voice recognition and allows customization based on individual user needs.
VietMap Live
VietMap Live is an intelligent navigation application optimized specifically for VinFast vehicles, helping users travel more conveniently, safely, and efficiently with continuously updated traffic data.
Feature Overview
Real-time traffic updates
Warnings for traffic cameras and traffic violation fines
Route optimization based on driving conditions
Integration with VinFast ecosystem and charging stations
Voice control support via the Smart Assistant
Usage Requirements
The vehicle must have an active VF eStore service package
The infotainment system must be functioning properly
The vehicle must have a stable Internet connection (Wi-Fi or mobile data)
The VietMap Live application must be installed on the vehicle
How to Open VietMap Live
Go to the App Library on the central display
Select VietMap Live
Tap Open to start using the application
For more details about VietMap Live, please refer to the official VietMap website.
Battery Care
Battery Care is a feature within the VF eStore smart services suite that helps users monitor battery health and identify usage behaviors that may affect the lifespan of an electric vehicle battery.
By analyzing real-world vehicle operating data, the feature provides detailed insights into battery status, charging behavior, driving behavior, and parking behavior. This enables users to proactively adjust their usage habits and perform battery maintenance or warranty services when necessary.
Key feature:
Monitor battery operating parameters and health status.
Detect behaviors that may reduce battery lifespan.
Identify early signs of battery abnormalities.
Adjust usage habits to optimize battery performance and longevity.
Reduce maintenance, repair, and battery replacement costs throughout vehicle ownership.
Usage Requirements
To use this feature, users must ensure that:
The vehicle has been updated to the latest FRS version.
The VinFast App has been updated to the latest version.
A Smart Connect Upper/Pro/Premium/Prime service package within the VF eStore smart services ecosystem has been activated.
Data sharing (Data Privacy) has been enabled on the vehicle.
User Guide
Accessing the Feature
Open the VinFast App.
Select Battery Care from the home screen.
Monitoring Battery Parameters
On the overview screen, users can monitor battery-related information, including:
Battery temperature.
Ambient temperature.
Battery technical specifications, such as:
Battery cell type.
Battery manufacturer.
Internal resistance.
Battery current.
Other technical parameters.
For each parameter, users can tap the (!) icon to view:
An explanation of the parameter.
Safe operating thresholds.
Warning thresholds that require attention.
Recommended maintenance and usage practices.
Monitoring Charging Behavior
Under the Charging Behavior section, users can view statistics related to charging habits that may affect battery lifespan, including:
requency of AC charging and DC charging.
Frequency of charging when the battery level is below 20%.
Frequency of charging when the battery level is above 80%.
Detailed information about the most recent charging session.
These insights help users identify and adjust suboptimal charging habits to extend battery lifespan.
Monitoring Driving and Parking Behavior
Under the Driving & Parking Behavior section, users can review vehicle operating habits that may impact battery health, including:
Frequency of aggressive acceleration.
Frequency of hard braking.
Frequency of parking the vehicle with a battery level below 10%.
Frequency of parking the vehicle with a battery level above 90%.
Frequency of parking the vehicle in environments with temperatures above 40°C.
These statistics help users better understand how their driving and parking habits affect battery longevity and make appropriate adjustments.
Monitoring Alerts and Recommendations
The system displays alerts or recommendations when it detects:
Battery parameters exceeding recommended thresholds.
Non-optimal charging behavior.
Driving or parking habits that may negatively impact battery lifespan.
Users are encouraged to follow the system’s recommendations to maintain battery performance and maximize battery lifespan throughout vehicle use.
Safe Driving
Feature Description
Safe Driving is a feature within the Safe Driving solution suite on the VinFast App that helps users monitor, evaluate, and improve their driving habits through a driving behavior scoring system.
The system analyzes vehicle operation data and evaluates driving safety based on seven key driving behaviors:
Hard braking
Aggressive steering
Following too closely
Speeding
Night driving
Autopilot forced disengagement
Not wearing a seat belt
Based on the collected data, the system calculates a driving score, provides historical performance records, and offers recommendations to help users develop safer driving habits.
This feature is currently supported on VinFast commercial vehicle models capable of providing vehicle operation data to the VinFast App.
Usage Requirements
To use this feature, users must ensure that:
The vehicle has been updated to the latest FRS version.
The VinFast App has been updated to the latest version.
Data sharing (Data Privacy) has been enabled on the vehicle so that the system can collect and analyze driving data.
User Guide
Accessing the Feature
Open the VinFast App.
Select Safe Driving.
Access the Safe Driving screen.
Viewing the Overall Driving Score
The overview screen displays:
An average driving score on a 100-point scale.
A score calculated based on the evaluation results of the seven driving behaviors.
A safety rating indicator classified into the following levels:
Dangerous
Average
Safe
Viewing Score History
Users can review their driving score history by:
Week
Month
Year
Historical data helps users assess trends in improving or changing driving behaviors over time.
Viewing Detailed Driving Behaviors
Under the Behavior section, users can view:
Scores for each driving behavior.
The number of corresponding violations.
Statistical charts over time.
The monitored driving behaviors include:
Hard braking
Aggressive steering
Following too closely
Speeding
Night driving
Autopilot forced disengagement
Not wearing a seat belt
Reviewing Safe Driving Recommendations
Under the Behavior section, the system provides personalized recommendations and safe driving guidance based on actual driving performance.
These recommendations help users:
Reduce the frequency of risky driving behaviors.
Improve their driving score.
Develop safer and more efficient driving habits.
